+60|
+61|  initializeCaret() {
+62|    this.carets = [{ offset: 0 }];
+63|    this.primaryCaretIndex = 0;
+64|    this.updateCaretInfo();
+65|  }
+66|
+67|  initializeEventListeners() {
+68|    // Editor events
+69|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.handleInput(e));
+70|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleKeyDown(e));
+71|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => this.handleKeyUp(e));
+72|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => this.handleClick(e));
+73|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => 
this.handleMouseDown(e));
+74|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => 
this.handleMouseMove(e));
+75|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => this.handleMouseUp(e));
+76|    this.editorEl.addEventListener('paste', (e) => this.handlePaste(e));
+77|
+78|    // Button events
+79|    document.getElementById('saveBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.save());
+80|    document.getElementById('undoBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.undo());
+81|    document.getElementById('redoBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.redo());
+82|    document.getElementById('findNextBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.findNext());
+83|    document.getElementById('findPrevBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.findPrev());
+84|    document.getElementById('replaceCurrBtn').addEventListener('click', () 
=> this.replaceCurrent());
+85|    document.getElementById('replaceAllBtn').addEventListener('click', () =>
this.replaceAll());
+86|    document.getElementById('historyBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.toggleHistory());
+87|    document.getElementById('closeHistoryBtn').addEventListener('click', () 
=> this.toggleHistory());
+88|
+89|    // Find input events
+90|    const findInput = document.getElementById('findInput');
+91|    findInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+92|      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
+93|        e.shiftKey ? this.findPrev() : this.findNext();
+94|      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+95|        this.editorEl.focus();
+96|      }
+97|    });
+98|    findInput.addEventListener('input', () => this.updateFind());
+99|
+100|    // Conflict modal
+101|    document.getElementById('discardBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.reloadDocument());
+102|    document.getElementById('reloadBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
window.location.reload());
+103|
+104|    // Load history
+105|    this.loadHistory();
+106|  }
+107|
+108|  getText() {
+109|    return this.editorEl.textContent;
+110|  }
+111|
+112|  setText(text) {
+113|    this.editorEl.textContent = text;
+114|  }
+115|
+116|  getCaretOffset(caret) {
+117|    if (!caret) caret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+118|    const selection = window.getSelection();
+119|    if (selection.rangeCount === 0) return 0;
+120|    const range = selection.getRangeAt(0);
+121|    return this.getOffsetFromNode(this.editorEl, range.startContainer, 
range.startOffset);
+122|  }
+123|
+124|  setCaretOffset(offset, caret) {
+125|    if (!caret) caret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+126|    caret.offset = offset;
+127|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+128|  }
+129|
+130|  getOffsetFromNode(root, node, offset) {
+131|    let totalOffset = 0;
+132|    let walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, 
null, false);
+133|
+134|    let currentNode;
+135|    while (currentNode = walker.nextNode()) {
+136|      if (currentNode === node) {
+137|        return totalOffset + offset;
+138|      }
+139|      totalOffset += currentNode.textContent.length;
+140|    }
+141|
+142|    return totalOffset;
+143|  }
+144|
+145|  getNodeAndOffsetFromOffset(root, targetOffset) {
+146|    let currentOffset = 0;
+147|    let walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, 
null, false);
+148|
+149|    let currentNode;
+150|    while (currentNode = walker.nextNode()) {
+151|      const length = currentNode.textContent.length;
+152|      if (currentOffset + length >= targetOffset) {
+153|        return { node: currentNode, offset: targetOffset - currentOffset };
+154|      }
+155|      currentOffset += length;
+156|    }
+157|
+158|    const lastNode = this.getLastTextNode(root);
+159|    return { node: lastNode || root, offset: 0 };
+160|  }
+161|
+162|  getLastTextNode(root) {
+163|    const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, 
null, false);
+164|    let lastNode = null;
+165|    let currentNode;
+166|    while (currentNode = walker.nextNode()) {
+167|      lastNode = currentNode;
+168|    }
+169|    return lastNode;
+170|  }
+171|
+172|  syncCaretToDOM() {
+173|    const selection = window.getSelection();
+174|    const primaryCaret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+175|    const { node, offset } = this.getNodeAndOffsetFromOffset(this.editorEl,
primaryCaret.offset);
+176|
+177|    if (node && node.parentNode) {
+178|      const range = document.createRange();
+179|      range.setStart(node, Math.min(offset, node.textContent.length));
+180|      range.collapse(true);
+181|      selection.removeAllRanges();
+182|      selection.addRange(range);
+183|    }
+184|
+185|    this.updateCaretInfo();
+186|  }
+187|
+188|  handleInput(e) {
+189|    e.preventDefault();
+190|    const newText = this.editorEl.textContent;
+191|    const oldText = this.history[this.historyIndex]?.text || 
this.currentDocument.content;
+192|
+193|    if (newText === oldText) return;
+194|
+195|    this.recordEdit(newText);
+196|    this.updateLineNumbers();
+197|    this.markModified();
+198|    this.updateCaretInfo();
+199|  }
+200|
+201|  handleKeyDown(e) {
+202|    const primaryCaret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+203|    const text = this.getText();
+204|
+205|    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
+206|      e.preventDefault();
+207|      this.insertText('\n');
+208|      return;
+209|    }
+210|
+211|    if (e.key === 'Tab') {
+212|      e.preventDefault();
+213|      const indent = e.shiftKey ? this.unindentAtCaret() : '\t';
+214|      if (!e.shiftKey) {
+215|        this.insertText(indent);
+216|      }
+217|      return;
+218|    }
+219|
+220|    if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
+221|      e.preventDefault();
+222|      this.backspace();
+223|      return;
+224|    }
+225|
+226|    if (e.key === 'Delete' && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
+227|      e.preventDefault();
+228|      this.delete();
+229|      return;
+230|    }
+231|
+232|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 's') {
+233|      e.preventDefault();
+234|      this.save();
+235|      return;
+236|    }
+237|
+238|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'z' && !e.shiftKey) {
+239|      e.preventDefault();
+240|      this.undo();
+241|      return;
+242|    }
+243|
+244|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key === 'y' || (e.shiftKey && e.key 
=== 'z'))) {
+245|      e.preventDefault();
+246|      this.redo();
+247|      return;
+248|    }
+249|
+250|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'f') {
+251|      e.preventDefault();
+252|      document.getElementById('findInput').focus();
+253|      document.getElementById('findInput').select();
+254|      return;
+255|    }
+256|
+257|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'h') {
+258|      e.preventDefault();
+259|      document.getElementById('findInput').focus();
+260|      document.getElementById('replaceInput').focus();
+261|      return;
+262|    }
+263|
+264|    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+265|      e.preventDefault();
+266|      document.getElementById('findInput').focus();
+267|      return;
+268|    }
+269|
+270|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'a') {
+271|      e.preventDefault();
+272|      this.selectAll();
+273|      return;
+274|    }
+275|
+276|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'c') {
+277|      e.preventDefault();
+278|      this.copy();
+279|      return;
+280|    }
+281|
+282|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'x') {
+283|      e.preventDefault();
+284|      this.cut();
+285|      return;
+286|    }
+287|
+288|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'v') {
+289|      e.preventDefault();
+290|      this.paste();
+291|      return;
+292|    }
+293|
+294|    // Word navigation
+295|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
+296|      e.preventDefault();
+297|      this.moveToNextWord(e.shiftKey);
+298|      return;
+299|    }
+300|
+301|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+302|      e.preventDefault();
+303|      this.moveToPrevWord(e.shiftKey);
+304|      return;
+305|    }
+306|
+307|    // Home/End
+308|    if (e.key === 'Home') {
+309|      e.preventDefault();
+310|      this.moveToLineStart(e.shiftKey);
+311|      return;
+312|    }
+313|
+314|    if (e.key === 'End') {
+315|      e.preventDefault();
+316|      this.moveToLineEnd(e.shiftKey);
+317|      return;
+318|    }
+319|
+320|    // Multi-caret support
+321|    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) && e.key === 'Enter') {
+322|      e.preventDefault();
+323|      this.addCaret();
+324|      return;
+325|    }
+326|
+327|    // Arrow keys with shift for selection
+328|    if (e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowDown' || 
+329|        e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowRight')) {
+330|      return; // Let default behavior handle selection
+331|    }
+332|  }
+333|
+334|  handleKeyUp(e) {
+335|    this.updateCaretInfo();
+336|    this.updateFind();
+337|  }
+338|
+339|  handleClick(e) {
+340|    if (e.altKey || e.ctrlKey) {
+341|      e.preventDefault();
+342|      this.addCaret();
+343|    }
+344|  }
+345|
+346|  handleMouseDown(e) {
+347|    if (e.detail === 3) {
+348|      // Triple click - select line
+349|      e.preventDefault();
+350|      this.selectLine();
+351|    } else if (e.detail === 2) {
+352|      // Double click - select word
+353|      e.preventDefault();
+354|      this.selectWord();
+355|    }
+356|  }
+357|
+358|  handleMouseMove(e) {
+359|    // Cursor drag selection
+360|  }
+361|
+362|  handleMouseUp(e) {
+363|    this.updateCaretInfo();
+364|  }
+365|
+366|  handlePaste(e) {
+367|    e.preventDefault();
+368|    const text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData('text');
+369|    this.insertText(text);
+370|  }
+371|
+372|  insertText(text) {
+373|    const oldText = this.getText();
+374|    const primaryCaret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+375|    const offset = primaryCaret.offset;
+376|
+377|    const newText = oldText.slice(0, offset) + text + 
oldText.slice(offset);
+378|    this.setText(newText);
+379|    this.setCaretOffset(offset + text.length, primaryCaret);
+380|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+381|
+382|    this.recordEdit(newText);
+383|    this.updateLineNumbers();
+384|    this.markModified();
+385|  }
+386|
+387|  backspace() {
+388|    const text = this.getText();
+389|    const primaryCaret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+390|    if (primaryCaret.offset === 0) return;
+391|
+392|    const offset = primaryCaret.offset;
+393|    const before = text.slice(0, offset - 1);
+394|    const after = text.slice(offset);
+395|    const newText = before + after;
+396|
+397|    this.setText(newText);
+398|    this.setCaretOffset(offset - 1, primaryCaret);
+399|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+400|
+401|    this.recordEdit(newText);
+402|    this.updateLineNumbers();
+403|    this.markModified();
+404|  }
+405|
+406|  delete() {
+407|    const text = this.getText();
+408|    const primaryCaret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+409|    if (primaryCaret.offset >= text.length) return;
+410|
+411|    const offset = primaryCaret.offset;
+412|    const before = text.slice(0, offset);
+413|    const after = text.slice(offset + 1);
+414|    const newText = before + after;
+415|
+416|    this.setText(newText);
+417|    this.setCaretOffset(offset, primaryCaret);
+418|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+419|
+420|    this.recordEdit(newText);
+421|    this.updateLineNumbers();
+422|    this.markModified();
+423|  }
+424|
+425|  selectAll() {
+426|    const text = this.getText();
+427|    this.primaryCaret.offset = 0;
+428|    const range = document.createRange();
+429|    const selection = window.getSelection();
+430|    range.selectNodeContents(this.editorEl);
+431|    selection.removeAllRanges();
+432|    selection.addRange(range);
+433|  }
+434|
+435|  selectWord() {
+436|    const text = this.getText();
+437|    const offset = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset;
+438|
+439|    let start = offset;
+440|    let end = offset;
+441|
+442|    while (start > 0 && /\w/.test(text[start - 1])) start--;
+443|    while (end < text.length && /\w/.test(text[end])) end++;
+444|
+445|    this.setSelection(start, end);
+446|  }
+447|
+448|  selectLine() {
+449|    const text = this.getText();
+450|    const offset = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset;
+451|
+452|    let start = offset;
+453|    let end = offset;
+454|
+455|    while (start > 0 && text[start - 1] !== '\n') start--;
+456|    while (end < text.length && text[end] !== '\n') end++;
+457|
+458|    this.setSelection(start, end);
+459|  }
+460|
+461|  setSelection(start, end) {
+462|    const { node: startNode, offset: startOffset } = 
this.getNodeAndOffsetFromOffset(this.editorEl, start);
+463|    const { node: endNode, offset: endOffset } = 
this.getNodeAndOffsetFromOffset(this.editorEl, end);
+464|
+465|    const selection = window.getSelection();
+466|    const range = document.createRange();
+467|
+468|    if (startNode && startNode.parentNode && endNode && endNode.parentNode)
{
+469|      range.setStart(startNode, Math.min(startOffset, 
startNode.textContent.length));
+470|      range.setEnd(endNode, Math.min(endOffset, 
endNode.textContent.length));
+471|      selection.removeAllRanges();
+472|      selection.addRange(range);
+473|    }
+474|  }
+475|
+476|  moveToNextWord(select) {
+477|    const text = this.getText();
+478|    let offset = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset;
+479|
+480|    while (offset < text.length && /\s/.test(text[offset])) offset++;
+481|    while (offset < text.length && /\w/.test(text[offset])) offset++;
+482|
+483|    this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset = offset;
+484|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+485|  }
+486|
+487|  moveToPrevWord(select) {
+488|    const text = this.getText();
+489|    let offset = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset;
+490|
+491|    if (offset > 0) offset--;
+492|    while (offset > 0 && /\w/.test(text[offset - 1])) offset--;
+493|
+494|    this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset = offset;
+495|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+496|  }
+497|
+498|  moveToLineStart(select) {
+499|    const text = this.getText();
+500|    let offset = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset;
+501|
+502|    while (offset > 0 && text[offset - 1] !== '\n') offset--;
+503|
+504|    this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset = offset;
+505|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+506|  }
+507|
+508|  moveToLineEnd(select) {
+509|    const text = this.getText();
+510|    let offset = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset;
+511|
+512|    while (offset < text.length && text[offset] !== '\n') offset++;
+513|
+514|    this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset = offset;
+515|    this.syncCaretToDOM();
+516|  }
+517|
+518|  addCaret() {
+519|    const offset = this.getCaretOffset();
+520|    this.carets.push({ offset });
+521|    this.primaryCaretIndex = this.carets.length - 1;
+522|  }
+523|
+524|  copy() {
+525|    const selection = window.getSelection();
+526|    if (!selection.toString().length) return;
+527|
+528|    const text = selection.toString();
+529|    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).catch(err => console.error('Copy 
failed:', err));
+530|  }
+531|
+532|  cut() {
+533|    this.copy();
+534|    // Handle deletion of selection
+535|    const text = this.getText();
+536|    const selection = window.getSelection();
+537|    if (selection.toString().length > 0) {
+538|      const range = selection.getRangeAt(0);
+539|      range.deleteContents();
+540|      this.recordEdit(this.getText());
+541|      this.updateLineNumbers();
+542|      this.markModified();
+543|    }
+544|  }
+545|
+546|  paste() {
+547|    navigator.clipboard.readText()
+548|      .then(text => this.insertText(text))
+549|      .catch(err => console.error('Paste failed:', err));
+550|  }
+551|
+552|  unindentAtCaret() {
+553|    const text = this.getText();
+554|    const primaryCaret = this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex];
+555|    const offset = primaryCaret.offset;
+556|    const lineStart = text.lastIndexOf('\n', offset - 1) + 1;
+557|
+558|    if (offset > lineStart && text[lineStart] === '\t') {
+559|      this.setText(text.slice(0, lineStart) + text.slice(lineStart + 1));
+560|      this.setCaretOffset(offset - 1, primaryCaret);
+561|      this.syncCaretToDOM();
+562|      this.recordEdit(this.getText());
+563|      return '';
+564|    }
+565|    return '\t';
+566|  }
+567|
+568|  recordEdit(newText) {
+569|    if (this.isUndoRedo) {
+570|      this.isUndoRedo = false;
+571|      return;
+572|    }
+573|
+574|    const now = Date.now();
+575|    const lastEdit = this.history[this.historyIndex];
+576|    const canGroup = lastEdit && (now - this.lastActionTime) < 500 && 
+577|                     this.lastAction === 'typing';
+578|
+579|    if (canGroup && this.historyIndex + 1 < this.history.length) {
+580|      // Replace the last grouped edit
+581|      this.historyIndex++;
+582|      this.history[this.historyIndex] = { text: newText };
+583|    } else {
+584|      // Create new history entry
+585|      this.history = this.history.slice(0, this.historyIndex + 1);
+586|      this.history.push({ text: newText });
+587|      this.historyIndex = this.history.length - 1;
+588|    }
+589|
+590|    this.lastAction = 'typing';
+591|    this.lastActionTime = now;
+592|
+593|    document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex <= 0;
+594|    document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;
+595|  }
+596|
+597|  undo() {
+598|    if (this.historyIndex > 0) {
+599|      this.historyIndex--;
+600|      this.isUndoRedo = true;
+601|      this.setText(this.history[this.historyIndex].text);
+602|      this.updateLineNumbers();
+603|      this.syncCaretToDOM();
+604|      this.markModified();
+605|      this.updateFind();
+606|
+607|      document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex <= 0;
+608|      document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = false;
+609|    }
+610|  }
+611|
+612|  redo() {
+613|    if (this.historyIndex < this.history.length - 1) {
+614|      this.historyIndex++;
+615|      this.isUndoRedo = true;
+616|      this.setText(this.history[this.historyIndex].text);
+617|      this.updateLineNumbers();
+618|      this.syncCaretToDOM();
+619|      this.markModified();
+620|      this.updateFind();
+621|
+622|      document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = false;
+623|      document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;
+624|    }
+625|  }
+626|
+627|  updateLineNumbers() {
+628|    const text = this.getText();
+629|    const lines = text.split('\n').length;
+630|    let lineNumbers = '';
+631|    for (let i = 1; i <= lines; i++) {
+632|      lineNumbers += i + '\n';
+633|    }
+634|    this.lineNumbersEl.textContent = lineNumbers;
+635|  }
+636|
+637|  updateCaretInfo() {
+638|    const text = this.getText();
+639|    const selection = window.getSelection();
+640|    let caretPos = 0;
+641|
+642|    if (selection.rangeCount > 0) {
+643|      const range = selection.getRangeAt(0);
+644|      caretPos = this.getOffsetFromNode(this.editorEl, 
range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
+645|    }
+646|
+647|    const beforeCaret = text.slice(0, caretPos);
+648|    const lines = beforeCaret.split('\n');
+649|    const lineNum = lines.length;
+650|    const col = lines[lines.length - 1].length + 1;
+651|
+652|    this.cursorInfoEl.textContent = `Line ${lineNum}, Column ${col}`;
+653|    this.carets[this.primaryCaretIndex].offset = caretPos;
+654|  }
+655|
+656|  updateFind() {
+657|    const query = document.getElementById('findInput').value;
+658|    if (!query) {
+659|      this.findMatches = [];
+660|      this.currentMatchIndex = -1;
+661|      document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = '';
+662|      return;
+663|    }
+664|
+665|    const text = this.getText();
+666|    const regex = new RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g');
+667|    this.findMatches = [];
+668|
+669|    let match;
+670|    while ((match = regex.exec(text)) !== null) {
+671|      this.findMatches.push({ index: match.index, length: match[0].length 
});
+672|    }
+673|
+674|    document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = 
+675|      `${this.findMatches.length} match${this.findMatches.length !== 1 ? 
'es' : ''}`;
+676|  }
+677|
+678|  findNext() {
+679|    if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;
+680|
+681|    this.currentMatchIndex = (this.currentMatchIndex + 1) % 
this.findMatches.length;
+682|    const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];
+683|    this.setSelection(match.index, match.index + match.length);
+684|  }
+685|
+686|  findPrev() {
+687|    if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;
+688|
+689|    this.currentMatchIndex = (this.currentMatchIndex - 1 + 
this.findMatches.length) % this.findMatches.length;
+690|    const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];
+691|    this.setSelection(match.index, match.index + match.length);
+692|  }
+693|
+694|  replaceCurrent() {
+695|    if (this.currentMatchIndex === -1 || this.findMatches.length === 0) 
return;
+696|
+697|    const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];
+698|    const replaceText = document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;
+699|    const text = this.getText();
+700|
+701|    const newText = text.slice(0, match.index) + replaceText + 
text.slice(match.index + match.length);
+702|    this.setText(newText);
+703|    this.recordEdit(newText);
+704|    this.updateLineNumbers();
+705|    this.markModified();
+706|
+707|    // Update find matches
+708|    this.updateFind();
+709|    if (this.findMatches.length > 0) {
+710|      this.findNext();
+711|    }
+712|  }
+713|
+714|  replaceAll() {
+715|    if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;
+716|
+717|    const query = document.getElementById('findInput').value;
+718|    const replaceText = document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;
+719|    let text = this.getText();
+720|
+721|    text = text.replace(new RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g'), 
replaceText);
+722|
+723|    this.setText(text);
+724|    this.recordEdit(text);
+725|    this.updateLineNumbers();
+726|    this.markModified();
+727|
+728|    this.updateFind();
+729|  }
+730|
+731|  escapeRegex(string) {
+732|    return string.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
+733|  }
+734|
+735|  toggleHistory() {
+736|    const panel = document.getElementById('historyPanel');
+737|    if (panel.style.display === 'none') {
+738|      panel.style.display = 'flex';
+739|      this.displayHistory();
+740|    } else {
+741|      panel.style.display = 'none';
+742|    }
+743|  }
+744|
+745|  loadHistory() {
+746|    // History is loaded with document
+747|  }
+748|
+749|  displayHistory() {
+750|    const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
+751|    historyList.innerHTML = '';
+752|
+753|    const revisions = this.currentDocument.revisions || [];
+754|    revisions.forEach(rev => {
+755|      const item = document.createElement('div');
+756|      item.className = 'history-item';
+757|      
+758|      const revNum = document.createElement('div');
+759|      revNum.className = 'history-item-revision';
+760|      revNum.textContent = `Revision ${rev.revision}`;
+761|      
+762|      const timestamp = document.createElement('div');
+763|      timestamp.className = 'history-item-timestamp';
+764|      const date = new Date(rev.timestamp);
+765|      timestamp.textContent = date.toLocaleString();
+766|      
+767|      const buttons = document.createElement('div');
+768|      buttons.className = 'history-item-buttons';
+769|      
+770|      const previewBtn = document.createElement('button');
+771|      previewBtn.className = 'btn btn-sm';
+772|      previewBtn.textContent = 'Preview';
+773|      previewBtn.addEventListener('click', () => 
this.previewRevision(rev.revision));
+774|      
+775|      const restoreBtn = document.createElement('button');
+776|      restoreBtn.className = 'btn btn-sm';
+777|      restoreBtn.textContent = 'Restore';
+778|      restoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => 
this.restoreRevision(rev.revision));
+779|      
+780|      buttons.appendChild(previewBtn);
+781|      buttons.appendChild(restoreBtn);
+782|      
+783|      item.appendChild(revNum);
+784|      item.appendChild(timestamp);
+785|      item.appendChild(buttons);
+786|      
+787|      historyList.appendChild(item);
+788|    });
+789|  }
+790|
+791|  async previewRevision(revision) {
+792|    try {
+793|      const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/revisions/${revision}`);
+794|      const data = await response.json();
+795|
+796|      const modal = document.getElementById('previewModal');
+797|      const content = document.getElementById('previewContent');
+798|      content.textContent = data.content;
+799|      modal.style.display = 'flex';
+800|    } catch (err) {
+801|      this.showError('Failed to load revision');
+802|    }
+803|  }
+804|
+805|  restoreRevision(revision) {
+806|    const closePreview = () => {
+807|      document.getElementById('previewModal').style.display = 'none';
+808|    };
+809|    
+810|    // Fetch the revision content
+811|    fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/revisions/${revision}`)
+812|      .then(r => r.json())
+813|      .then(data => {
+814|        closePreview();
+815|        this.setText(data.content);
+816|        this.recordEdit(data.content);
+817|        this.updateLineNumbers();
+818|        this.markModified();
+819|        this.initializeCaret();
+820|        this.showMessage(`Restored revision ${revision} as unsaved 
changes`);
+821|      })
+822|      .catch(err => this.showError('Failed to restore revision'));
+823|  }
+824|
+825|  async save() {
+826|    if (this.syncInProgress) return;
+827|    
+828|    const content = this.getText();
+829|    const currentDoc = this.currentDocument.content;
+830|
+831|    if (content === currentDoc) {
+832|      this.showMessage('No changes to save');
+833|      return;
+834|    }
+835|
+836|    this.syncInProgress = true;
+837|    document.getElementById('saveBtn').disabled = true;
+838|
+839|    try {
+840|      const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/save`, {
+841|        method: 'POST',
+842|        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+843|        body: JSON.stringify({
+844|          content,
+845|          baseRevision: this.baseRevision,
+846|          documentId: this.currentDocument.id
+847|        })
+848|      });
+849|
+850|      const data = await response.json();
+851|
+852|      if (response.status === 409) {
+853|        // Conflict detected
+854|        this.showConflict(data.currentRevision);
+855|        return;
+856|      }
+857|
+858|      if (!response.ok) {
+859|        throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed');
+860|      }
+861|
+862|      // Success
+863|      this.currentDocument.currentRevision = data.revision;
+864|      this.baseRevision = data.revision;
+865|      this.currentDocument.content = content;
+866|      this.revisionEl.textContent = `Revision ${data.revision}`;
+867|      this.markSaved();
+868|      this.showMessage('Saved successfully');
+869|
+870|      // Reload history
+871|      this.loadDocument();
+872|    } catch (err) {
+873|      this.showError(err.message || 'Save failed');
+874|    } finally {
+875|      this.syncInProgress = false;
+876|      document.getElementById('saveBtn').disabled = false;
+877|    }
+878|  }
+879|
+880|  async reloadDocument() {
+881|    try {
+882|      const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}`);
+883|      const doc = await response.json();
+884|
+885|      this.currentDocument = doc;
+886|      this.baseRevision = doc.currentRevision;
+887|      this.setText(doc.content);
+888|      this.initializeCaret();
+889|      this.updateLineNumbers();
+890|      this.markSaved();
+891|
+892|      document.getElementById('conflictModal').style.display = 'none';
+893|      this.showMessage('Document reloaded from server');
+894|    } catch (err) {
+895|      this.showError('Failed to reload document');
+896|    }
+897|  }
+898|
+899|  showConflict(serverRevision) {
+900|    this.saveStatusEl.classList.add('error');
+901|    this.statusEl.textContent = 'Conflict: Save rejected';
+902|    document.getElementById('conflictModal').style.display = 'flex';
+903|  }
+904|
+905|  markModified() {
+906|    this.isModified = true;
+907|    this.saveStatusEl.classList.add('unsaved');
+908|    this.statusEl.textContent = 'Unsaved changes';
+909|  }
+910|
+911|  markSaved() {
+912|    this.isModified = false;
+913|    this.saveStatusEl.classList.remove('unsaved', 'error');
+914|    this.statusEl.textContent = 'Saved';
+915|  }
+916|
+917|  showMessage(message) {
+918|    const statusBar = document.getElementById('statusBar');
+919|    statusBar.textContent = message;
+920|    setTimeout(() => {
+921|      if (statusBar.textContent === message) {
+922|        statusBar.textContent = '';
+923|      }
+924|    }, 3000);
+925|  }
+926|
+927|  showError(message) {
+928|    const statusBar = document.getElementById('statusBar');
+929|    statusBar.textContent = '✗ ' + message;
+930|    statusBar.style.color = 'var(--color-error)';
+931|    setTimeout(() => {
+932|      statusBar.textContent = '';
+933|      statusBar.style.color = '';
+934|    }, 5000);
+935|  }
+936|}
