if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'f') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      
document.getElementById('findInput').focus();\n      
document.getElementById('findInput').select();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if 
(ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'h') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      
document.getElementById('replaceInput').focus();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if
(ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'a') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      
this.selectAll();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'c') 
{\n      e.preventDefault();\n      this.copy();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if
(ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'x') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      this.cut();\n  
return;\n    }\n\n    if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'v') {\n      
e.preventDefault();\n      this.paste();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (e.key 
=== 'Escape') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      this.clearSelection();\n      
document.getElementById('findInput').focus();\n      return;\n    }\n  }\n\n  
handleKeyUp(e) {\n    this.updateCaretInfo();\n  }\n\n  handleInput(e) {\n    //
Clear the input\n    this.editorInput.value = '';\n  }\n\n  async handlePaste(e)
{\n    e.preventDefault();\n    try {\n      const text = await 
navigator.clipboard.readText();\n      this.insertChar(text);\n    } catch (err)
{\n      console.error('Paste failed:', err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  
handleDisplayClick(e) {\n    const offset = this.getOffsetFromClick(e);\n    
this.caretOffset = offset;\n    this.clearSelection();\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n    this.editorInput.focus();\n  }\n\n  
handleDisplayMouseDown(e) {\n    this.mouseDownOffset = 
this.getOffsetFromClick(e);\n    this.selectionStart = this.mouseDownOffset;\n  
this.selectionEnd = this.mouseDownOffset;\n    this.caretOffset = 
this.mouseDownOffset;\n  }\n\n  handleDisplayMouseMove(e) {\n    if (typeof 
this.mouseDownOffset !== 'number') return;\n    const offset = 
this.getOffsetFromClick(e);\n    this.selectionEnd = offset;\n    if 
(this.selectionStart > this.selectionEnd) {\n      [this.selectionStart, 
this.selectionEnd] = [this.selectionEnd, this.selectionStart];\n    }\n    
this.caretOffset = offset;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  
handleDisplayMouseUp(e) {\n    delete this.mouseDownOffset;\n  }\n\n  
getOffsetFromClick(e) {\n    const rect = 
this.editorDisplay.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const x = e.clientX - 
rect.left;\n    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n\n    const lines = 
this.text.split('\\n');\n    let offset = 0;\n    const lineHeight = 
parseFloat(getComputedStyle(this.editorDisplay).lineHeight);\n    const 
charWidth = 8; // Approximate, monospace font\n\n    let lineNum = Math.floor((y
+ this.editorContent.scrollTop) / lineHeight);\n    lineNum = Math.max(0, 
Math.min(lineNum, lines.length - 1));\n\n    // Add all previous lines\n    for 
(let i = 0; i < lineNum; i++) {\n      offset += lines[i].length + 1; // +1 for 
newline\n    }\n\n    // Add position in current line\n    const charPos = 
Math.round((x + this.editorContent.scrollLeft) / charWidth);\n    const 
lineLength = lines[lineNum].length;\n    offset += Math.max(0, Math.min(charPos,
lineLength));\n\n    return Math.min(offset, this.text.length);\n  }\n\n  
insertChar(char) {\n    if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== 
-1) {\n      // Replace selection\n      const start = 
Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      const end = 
Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      this.text = 
this.text.slice(0, start) + char + this.text.slice(end);\n      this.caretOffset
= start + char.length;\n      this.clearSelection();\n    } else {\n      // 
Insert at caret\n      this.text = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset) + char +
this.text.slice(this.caretOffset);\n      this.caretOffset += char.length;\n    
}\n\n    this.recordEdit();\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.updateLineNumbers();\n    this.markModified();\n  }\n\n  backspace() {\n   
if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {\n      // Delete 
selection\n      const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, 
this.selectionEnd);\n      this.text = this.text.slice(0, start) + 
this.text.slice(Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd));\n      
this.caretOffset = start;\n      this.clearSelection();\n    } else if 
(this.caretOffset > 0) {\n      this.text = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset 
- 1) + this.text.slice(this.caretOffset);\n      this.caretOffset--;\n    }\n\n 
this.recordEdit();\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    this.updateLineNumbers();\n  
this.markModified();\n  }\n\n  delete() {\n    if (this.selectionStart !== -1 &&
this.selectionEnd !== -1) {\n      const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, 
this.selectionEnd);\n      this.text = this.text.slice(0, start) + 
this.text.slice(Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd));\n      
this.caretOffset = start;\n      this.clearSelection();\n    } else if 
(this.caretOffset < this.text.length) {\n      this.text = this.text.slice(0, 
this.caretOffset) + this.text.slice(this.caretOffset + 1);\n    }\n\n    
this.recordEdit();\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    this.updateLineNumbers();\n  
this.markModified();\n  }\n\n  moveRight() {\n    this.caretOffset = 
Math.min(this.caretOffset + 1, this.text.length);\n    this.clearSelection();\n 
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  moveLeft() {\n    this.caretOffset = 
Math.max(this.caretOffset - 1, 0);\n    this.clearSelection();\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  moveUp() {\n    const lineStart = 
this.text.lastIndexOf('\\n', this.caretOffset - 1) + 1;\n    if (lineStart === 
0) return; // Already on first line\n\n    const colInLine = this.caretOffset - 
lineStart;\n    const prevLineStart = this.text.lastIndexOf('\\n', lineStart - 
2) + 1;\n    const prevLineEnd = lineStart - 1;\n    const prevLineLength = 
prevLineEnd - prevLineStart;\n\n    this.caretOffset = prevLineStart + 
Math.min(colInLine, prevLineLength);\n    this.clearSelection();\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  moveDown() {\n    const lineStart = 
this.text.lastIndexOf('\\n', this.caretOffset - 1) + 1;\n    const lineEnd = 
this.text.indexOf('\\n', this.caretOffset);\n    const actualLineEnd = lineEnd 
=== -1 ? this.text.length : lineEnd;\n\n    if (actualLineEnd === 
this.text.length) return; // Already on last line\n\n    const colInLine = 
this.caretOffset - lineStart;\n    const nextLineStart = actualLineEnd + 1;\n   
const nextLineEnd = this.text.indexOf('\\n', nextLineStart);\n    const 
actualNextLineEnd = nextLineEnd === -1 ? this.text.length : nextLineEnd;\n    
const nextLineLength = actualNextLineEnd - nextLineStart;\n\n    
this.caretOffset = nextLineStart + Math.min(colInLine, nextLineLength);\n    
this.clearSelection();\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  
moveToLineStart(select) {\n    const lineStart = this.text.lastIndexOf('\\n', 
this.caretOffset - 1) + 1;\n    if (select) {\n      if (this.selectionStart ===
-1) {\n        this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;\n      }\n      
this.selectionEnd = lineStart;\n    } else {\n      this.clearSelection();\n    
}\n    this.caretOffset = lineStart;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  
moveToLineEnd(select) {\n    const lineEnd = this.text.indexOf('\\n', 
this.caretOffset);\n    const actualLineEnd = lineEnd === -1 ? this.text.length 
: lineEnd;\n    if (select) {\n      if (this.selectionStart === -1) {\n        
this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;\n      }\n      this.selectionEnd = 
actualLineEnd;\n    } else {\n      this.clearSelection();\n    }\n    
this.caretOffset = actualLineEnd;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  
moveToNextWord(select) {\n    let offset = this.caretOffset;\n    // Skip 
current word\n    while (offset < this.text.length && 
/\\w/.test(this.text[offset])) offset++;\n    // Skip whitespace\n    while 
(offset < this.text.length && /\\s/.test(this.text[offset])) offset++;\n\n    if
(select) {\n      if (this.selectionStart === -1) {\n        this.selectionStart
= this.caretOffset;\n      }\n      this.selectionEnd = offset;\n    } else {\n 
this.clearSelection();\n    }\n    this.caretOffset = offset;\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  moveToPrevWord(select) {\n    let offset = 
this.caretOffset - 1;\n    if (offset < 0) return;\n\n    // Skip whitespace 
backward\n    while (offset >= 0 && /\\s/.test(this.text[offset])) offset--;\n  
// Skip word backward\n    while (offset >= 0 && /\\w/.test(this.text[offset])) 
offset--;\n    offset++; // Back to start of word\n\n    if (select) {\n      if
(this.selectionStart === -1) {\n        this.selectionStart = 
this.caretOffset;\n      }\n      this.selectionEnd = offset;\n    } else {\n   
this.clearSelection();\n    }\n    this.caretOffset = offset;\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  extendSelectionRight() {\n    if 
(this.selectionStart === -1) {\n      this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;\n 
this.selectionEnd = this.caretOffset + 1;\n    } else {\n      
this.selectionEnd++;\n    }\n    this.caretOffset = this.selectionEnd;\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  extendSelectionLeft() {\n    if 
(this.selectionStart === -1) {\n      this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;\n 
this.selectionEnd = this.caretOffset - 1;\n    } else {\n      
this.selectionEnd--;\n    }\n    this.caretOffset = this.selectionEnd;\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  extendSelectionUp() {\n    // Complex - need to 
track column position\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  extendSelectionDown()
{\n    // Complex - need to track column position\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  
}\n\n  selectAll() {\n    this.selectionStart = 0;\n    this.selectionEnd = 
this.text.length;\n    this.caretOffset = this.text.length;\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  clearSelection() {\n    this.selectionStart = 
-1;\n    this.selectionEnd = -1;\n  }\n\n  copy() {\n    if (this.selectionStart
!== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {\n      const start = 
Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      const end = 
Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      const selectedText = 
this.text.slice(start, end);\n      
navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedText).catch(err => console.error('Copy 
failed:', err));\n    }\n  }\n\n  cut() {\n    if (this.selectionStart !== -1 &&
this.selectionEnd !== -1) {\n      this.copy();\n      const start = 
Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      const end = 
Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      this.text = 
this.text.slice(0, start) + this.text.slice(end);\n      this.caretOffset = 
start;\n      this.clearSelection();\n      this.recordEdit();\n      
this.updateDisplay();\n      this.updateLineNumbers();\n      
this.markModified();\n    }\n  }\n\n  paste() {\n    
navigator.clipboard.readText()\n      .then(text => this.insertChar(text))\n    
.catch(err => console.error('Paste failed:', err));\n  }\n\n  recordEdit() {\n  
if (this.isUndoRedo) {\n      this.isUndoRedo = false;\n      return;\n    }\n\n
const now = Date.now();\n    const canGroup = this.lastAction === 'typing' && 
(now - this.lastActionTime) < 500;\n\n    if (canGroup && this.historyIndex < 
this.history.length - 1) {\n      // Replace the last history entry\n      
this.history[this.historyIndex] = this.text;\n    } else {\n      // Create new 
history entry\n      this.history = this.history.slice(0, this.historyIndex + 
1);\n      this.history.push(this.text);\n      this.historyIndex++;\n    }\n\n 
this.lastAction = 'typing';\n    this.lastActionTime = now;\n\n    
document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex <= 0;\n    
document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;\n  }\n\n  undo() {\n    if (this.historyIndex > 0) {\n 
this.historyIndex--;\n      this.isUndoRedo = true;\n      this.text = 
this.history[this.historyIndex];\n      this.clearSelection();\n      
this.updateDisplay();\n      this.updateLineNumbers();\n      
this.markModified();\n\n      document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = 
this.historyIndex <= 0;\n      document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = 
false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  redo() {\n    if (this.historyIndex < 
this.history.length - 1) {\n      this.historyIndex++;\n      this.isUndoRedo = 
true;\n      this.text = this.history[this.historyIndex];\n      
this.clearSelection();\n      this.updateDisplay();\n      
this.updateLineNumbers();\n      this.markModified();\n\n      
document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = false;\n      
document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;\n    }\n  }\n\n  updateDisplay() {\n    const html = 
this.renderContent();\n    this.editorDisplay.innerHTML = html;\n  }\n\n  
renderContent() {\n    let html = '';\n    let offset = 0;\n\n    for (let i = 
0; i < this.text.length; i++) {\n      const char = this.text[i];\n      let 
charHtml = this.escapeHtml(char);\n\n      // Check if this position is in 
selection\n      if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {\n
const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n        const 
end = Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n        if (i >= start 
&& i < end) {\n          charHtml = `<span 
class=\"selection\">${charHtml}</span>`;\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Check 
if this is a find match\n      for (let m = 0; m < this.findMatches.length; m++)
{\n        const match = this.findMatches[m];\n        if (i >= match.index && i
< match.index + match.length) {\n          const isCurrent = m === 
this.currentMatchIndex;\n          charHtml = `<span 
class=\"find-match${isCurrent ? ' current' : ''}\">${charHtml}</span>`;\n       
break;\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Add caret at this position\n      if (i 
=== this.caretOffset) {\n        charHtml = `<span 
class=\"caret${this.caretVisible ? '' : ' hidden'}\">${charHtml || 
'|'}</span>${charHtml ? '' : ''}`;\n      }\n\n      if (char === '\\n') {\n    
html += '\\n';\n      } else if (char === '\\t') {\n        html += '  '; // Tab
as spaces for display\n      } else {\n        html += charHtml;\n      }\n\n   
offset++;\n    }\n\n    // Caret at end of text\n    if (this.caretOffset === 
this.text.length) {\n      html += `<span class=\"caret${this.caretVisible ? '' 
: ' hidden'}\">|</span>`;\n    }\n\n    return html;\n  }\n\n  escapeHtml(text) 
{\n    const map = {\n      '&': '&amp;',\n      '<': '&lt;',\n      '>': 
'&gt;',\n      '\"': '&quot;',\n      \"'\": '&#039;'\n    };\n    return 
text.replace(/[&<>\"']/g, m => map[m]);\n  }\n\n  updateLineNumbers() {\n    
const lines = this.text.split('\\n');\n    let html = '';\n    for (let i = 1; i
<= lines.length; i++) {\n      html += i + '\\n';\n    }\n    
this.lineNumbersEl.textContent = html;\n  }\n\n  updateCaretInfo() {\n    const 
beforeCaret = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset);\n    const lines = 
beforeCaret.split('\\n');\n    const lineNum = lines.length;\n    const col = 
lines[lines.length - 1].length + 1;\n\n    this.cursorInfoEl.textContent = `Line
${lineNum}, Column ${col}`;\n  }\n\n  updateFind() {\n    const query = 
document.getElementById('findInput').value;\n    if (!query) {\n      
this.findMatches = [];\n      this.currentMatchIndex = -1;\n      
document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = '';\n      
this.updateDisplay();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const regex = new 
RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g');\n    this.findMatches = [];\n\n    let 
match;\n    while ((match = regex.exec(this.text)) !== null) {\n      
this.findMatches.push({ index: match.index, length: match[0].length });\n    
}\n\n    document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = \n      
`${this.findMatches.length} match${this.findMatches.length !== 1 ? 'es' : 
''}`;\n\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  findNext() {\n    if 
(this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    this.currentMatchIndex = 
(this.currentMatchIndex + 1) % this.findMatches.length;\n    const match = 
this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];\n    this.selectionStart = 
match.index;\n    this.selectionEnd = match.index + match.length;\n    
this.caretOffset = match.index + match.length;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.editorInput.focus();\n  }\n\n  findPrev() {\n    if 
(this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    this.currentMatchIndex = 
(this.currentMatchIndex - 1 + this.findMatches.length) % 
this.findMatches.length;\n    const match = 
this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];\n    this.selectionStart = 
match.index;\n    this.selectionEnd = match.index + match.length;\n    
this.caretOffset = match.index + match.length;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.editorInput.focus();\n  }\n\n  replaceCurrent() {\n    if 
(this.currentMatchIndex === -1 || this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    
const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];\n    const replaceText =
document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;\n\n    this.text = 
this.text.slice(0, match.index) + replaceText + this.text.slice(match.index + 
match.length);\n    this.caretOffset = match.index + replaceText.length;\n    
this.clearSelection();\n    this.recordEdit();\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.updateLineNumbers();\n    this.markModified();\n\n    this.updateFind();\n 
if (this.findMatches.length > 0) {\n      this.findNext();\n    }\n  }\n\n  
replaceAll() {\n    if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    const 
query = document.getElementById('findInput').value;\n    const replaceText = 
document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;\n\n    this.text = 
this.text.replace(new RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g'), replaceText);\n    
this.clearSelection();\n    this.recordEdit();\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.updateLineNumbers();\n    this.markModified();\n\n    this.updateFind();\n 
}\n\n  escapeRegex(string) {\n    return 
string.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, '\\\\$&');\n  }\n\n  toggleHistory() 
{\n    const panel = document.getElementById('historyPanel');\n    if 
(panel.style.display === 'none') {\n      panel.style.display = 'flex';\n      
this.displayHistory();\n    } else {\n      panel.style.display = 'none';\n    
}\n  }\n\n  displayHistory() {\n    const historyList = 
document.getElementById('historyList');\n    historyList.innerHTML = '';\n\n    
const revisions = this.currentDocument.revisions || [];\n    
revisions.forEach(rev => {\n      const item = document.createElement('div');\n 
item.className = 'history-item';\n\n      const revNum = 
document.createElement('div');\n      revNum.className = 
'history-item-revision';\n      revNum.textContent = `Revision 
${rev.revision}`;\n\n      const timestamp = document.createElement('div');\n   
timestamp.className = 'history-item-timestamp';\n      const date = new 
Date(rev.timestamp);\n      timestamp.textContent = date.toLocaleString();\n\n  
const buttons = document.createElement('div');\n      buttons.className = 
'history-item-buttons';\n\n      const previewBtn = 
document.createElement('button');\n      previewBtn.className = 'btn btn-sm';\n 
previewBtn.textContent = 'Preview';\n      previewBtn.addEventListener('click', 
() => this.previewRevision(rev.revision));\n\n      const restoreBtn = 
document.createElement('button');\n      restoreBtn.className = 'btn btn-sm';\n 
restoreBtn.textContent = 'Restore';\n      restoreBtn.addEventListener('click', 
() => this.restoreRevision(rev.revision));\n\n      
buttons.appendChild(previewBtn);\n      buttons.appendChild(restoreBtn);\n\n    
item.appendChild(revNum);\n      item.appendChild(timestamp);\n      
item.appendChild(buttons);\n\n      historyList.appendChild(item);\n    });\n  
}\n\n  async previewRevision(revision) {\n    try {\n      const response = 
await fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/revisions/${revision}`);\n 
const data = await response.json();\n\n      const modal = 
document.getElementById('previewModal');\n      const content = 
document.getElementById('previewContent');\n      content.textContent = 
data.content;\n      modal.style.display = 'flex';\n    } catch (err) {\n      
this.showError('Failed to load revision');\n    }\n  }\n\n  
restoreRevision(revision) {\n    const closePreview = () => {\n      
document.getElementById('previewModal').style.display = 'none';\n    };\n\n    
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/revisions/${revision}`)\n      
.then(r => r.json())\n      .then(data => {\n        closePreview();\n        
this.text = data.content;\n        this.recordEdit();\n        this.caretOffset 
= 0;\n        this.clearSelection();\n        this.updateDisplay();\n        
this.updateLineNumbers();\n        this.markModified();\n        
this.showMessage(`Restored revision ${revision} as unsaved changes`);\n      
})\n      .catch(err => this.showError('Failed to restore revision'));\n  }\n\n 
async save() {\n    if (this.syncInProgress) return;\n\n    const content = 
this.text;\n    const currentDoc = this.currentDocument.content;\n\n    if 
(content === currentDoc) {\n      this.showMessage('No changes to save');\n     
return;\n    }\n\n    this.syncInProgress = true;\n    
document.getElementById('saveBtn').disabled = true;\n\n    try {\n      const 
response = await fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/save`, {\n      
method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n     
body: JSON.stringify({\n          content,\n          baseRevision: 
this.baseRevision,\n          documentId: this.currentDocument.id\n        })\n 
});\n\n      const data = await response.json();\n\n      if (response.status 
=== 409) {\n        this.showConflict(data.currentRevision);\n        return;\n 
}\n\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        throw new Error(data.error || 'Save 
failed');\n      }\n\n      this.currentDocument.currentRevision = 
data.revision;\n      this.baseRevision = data.revision;\n      
this.currentDocument.content = content;\n      this.revisionEl.textContent = 
`Revision ${data.revision}`;\n      this.markSaved();\n      
this.showMessage('Saved successfully');\n\n      this.loadDocument();\n    } 
catch (err) {\n      this.showError(err.message || 'Save failed');\n    } 
finally {\n      this.syncInProgress = false;\n      
document.getElementById('saveBtn').disabled = false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  async 
reloadDocument() {\n    try {\n      const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}`);\n      const doc = await 
response.json();\n\n      this.currentDocument = doc;\n      this.baseRevision =
doc.currentRevision;\n      this.text = doc.content;\n      this.caretOffset = 
0;\n      this.clearSelection();\n      this.updateDisplay();\n      
this.updateLineNumbers();\n      this.markSaved();\n\n      
document.getElementById('conflictModal').style.display = 'none';\n      
this.showMessage('Document reloaded from server');\n    } catch (err) {\n      
this.showError('Failed to reload document');\n    }\n  }\n\n  
showConflict(serverRevision) {\n    this.saveStatusEl.classList.add('error');\n 
this.statusEl.textContent = 'Conflict: Save rejected';\n    
document.getElementById('conflictModal').style.display = 'flex';\n  }\n\n  
markModified() {\n    this.isModified = true;\n    
this.saveStatusEl.classList.add('unsaved');\n    this.statusEl.textContent = 
'Unsaved changes';\n  }\n\n  markSaved() {\n    this.isModified = false;\n    
this.saveStatusEl.classList.remove('unsaved', 'error');\n    
this.statusEl.textContent = 'Saved';\n  }\n\n  startCaretBlink() {\n    if 
(this.caretBlinkInterval) return;\n\n    this.caretBlinkInterval = 
setInterval(() => {\n      this.caretVisible = !this.caretVisible;\n      
this.updateDisplay();\n    }, 500);\n  }\n\n  showMessage(message) {\n    const 
statusBar = document.getElementById('statusBar');\n    statusBar.textContent = 
message;\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      if (statusBar.textContent === message) 
{\n        statusBar.textContent = '';\n      }\n    }, 3000);\n  }\n\n  
showError(message) {\n    const statusBar = 
document.getElementById('statusBar');\n    statusBar.textContent = '\u2717 ' + 
message;\n    statusBar.style.color = 'var(--color-error)';\n    setTimeout(() 
=> {\n      statusBar.textContent = '';\n      statusBar.style.color = '';\n    
}, 5000);\n  }\n}\n\n// Initialize editor\nlet 
editor;\ndocument.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n  editor = new 
TextEditor();\n});\n\nfunction closePreview() {\n  
document.getElementById('previewModal').style.display = 'none';\n}\n"}

Reasoning:
Let me rewrite the entire editor.js file with a better approach that doesn't use
contenteditable.

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/editor.js"
  file_text: 
    // PatchPad Editor - Custom DOM-based text editor
    class TextEditor {
      constructor() {
        this.editorInput = document.getElementById('editor');
        this.editorDisplay = document.getElementById('editorDisplay');
        this.editorContent = document.getElementById('editorContent');
        this.lineNumbersEl = document.getElementById('lineNumbers');
        this.statusEl = document.getElementById('statusText');
        this.revisionEl = document.getElementById('revisionText');
        this.cursorInfoEl = document.getElementById('cursorInfo');
        this.saveStatusEl = document.getElementById('saveStatus');
    
        // Content state
        this.text = '';
        this.currentDocument = null;
        this.baseRevision = null;
    
        // Edit history
        this.history = [];
        this.historyIndex = -1;
        this.isUndoRedo = false;
        this.lastAction = null;
        this.lastActionTime = 0;
    
        // Cursor and selection
        this.caretOffset = 0;
        this.selectionStart = -1;
        this.selectionEnd = -1;
    
        // Find/Replace
        this.findMatches = [];
        this.currentMatchIndex = -1;
        this.isModified = false;
        this.syncInProgress = false;
    
        // Caret visibility animation
        this.caretVisible = true;
        this.caretBlinkInterval = null;
    
        this.initializeEventListeners();
        this.loadDocument();
      }
    
      async loadDocument() {
        try {
          const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
          const docId = urlParams.get('doc') || 'incident-alpha';
    
          const response = await fetch(`/api/reports/${docId}`);
          if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error('Failed to load document');
          }
    
          this.currentDocument = await response.json();
          this.baseRevision = this.currentDocument.currentRevision;
          this.text = this.currentDocument.content;
    
          // Update UI
          document.getElementById('reportTitle').textContent = 
this.currentDocument.title;
          document.getElementById('authorInfo').textContent = `By 
${this.currentDocument.author}`;
          this.revisionEl.textContent = `Revision 
${this.currentDocument.currentRevision}`;
    
          this.updateDisplay();
          this.updateLineNumbers();
          this.markSaved();
          this.startCaretBlink();
          this.editorInput.focus();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to load document:', err);
          this.showError('Failed to load document');
        }
      }
    
      initializeEventListeners() {
        // Capture all keyboard input through hidden input
        this.editorInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => 
this.handleKeyDown(e));
        this.editorInput.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => this.handleKeyUp(e));
        this.editorInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.handleInput(e));
        this.editorInput.addEventListener('paste', (e) => this.handlePaste(e));
    
        // Click to position caret in display area
        this.editorDisplay.addEventListener('click', (e) => 
this.handleDisplayClick(e));
        this.editorDisplay.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => 
this.handleDisplayMouseDown(e));
        this.editorDisplay.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => 
this.handleDisplayMouseMove(e));
        this.editorDisplay.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => 
this.handleDisplayMouseUp(e));
    
        // Button events
        document.getElementById('saveBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.save());
        document.getElementById('undoBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.undo());
        document.getElementById('redoBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.redo());
        document.getElementById('findNextBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.findNext());
        document.getElementById('findPrevBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.findPrev());
        document.getElementById('replaceCurrBtn').addEventListener('click', () 
=> this.replaceCurrent());
        document.getElementById('replaceAllBtn').addEventListener('click', () =>
this.replaceAll());
        document.getElementById('historyBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.toggleHistory());
        document.getElementById('closeHistoryBtn').addEventListener('click', () 
=> this.toggleHistory());
    
        // Find input events
        const findInput = document.getElementById('findInput');
        findInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.shiftKey ? this.findPrev() : this.findNext();
          } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            this.editorInput.focus();
          }
        });
        findInput.addEventListener('input', () => this.updateFind());
    
        // Conflict modal
        document.getElementById('discardBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
this.reloadDocument());
        document.getElementById('reloadBtn').addEventListener('click', () => 
window.location.reload());
    
        // Click on editor content to focus
        this.editorDisplay.addEventListener('click', () => {
          this.editorInput.focus();
        });
      }
    
      handleKeyDown(e) {
        // Clear input after handling
        const key = e.key;
        const ctrlOrCmd = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
        const shift = e.shiftKey;
    
        // Text input (regular characters)
        if (!ctrlOrCmd && !e.altKey && e.key.length === 1) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.insertChar(e.key);
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.insertChar('\n');
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'Tab') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.insertChar('\t');
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.backspace();
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'Delete') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.delete();
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (ctrlOrCmd) {
            this.moveToNextWord(shift);
          } else if (shift) {
            this.extendSelectionRight();
          } else {
            this.moveRight();
          }
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (ctrlOrCmd) {
            this.moveToPrevWord(shift);
          } else if (shift) {
            this.extendSelectionLeft();
          } else {
            this.moveLeft();
          }
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (shift) {
            this.extendSelectionUp();
          } else {
            this.moveUp();
          }
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (shift) {
            this.extendSelectionDown();
          } else {
            this.moveDown();
          }
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'Home') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.moveToLineStart(shift);
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'End') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.moveToLineEnd(shift);
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 's') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.save();
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'z' && !shift) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.undo();
          return;
        }
    
        if ((ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'y') || (ctrlOrCmd && shift && e.key === 
'z')) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.redo();
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'f') {
          e.preventDefault();
          document.getElementById('findInput').focus();
          document.getElementById('findInput').select();
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'h') {
          e.preventDefault();
          document.getElementById('replaceInput').focus();
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'a') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.selectAll();
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'c') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.copy();
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'x') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.cut();
          return;
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.key === 'v') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.paste();
          return;
        }
    
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.clearSelection();
          document.getElementById('findInput').focus();
          return;
        }
      }
    
      handleKeyUp(e) {
        this.updateCaretInfo();
      }
    
      handleInput(e) {
        // Clear the input
        this.editorInput.value = '';
      }
    
      async handlePaste(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        try {
          const text = await navigator.clipboard.readText();
          this.insertChar(text);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Paste failed:', err);
        }
      }
    
      handleDisplayClick(e) {
        const offset = this.getOffsetFromClick(e);
        this.caretOffset = offset;
        this.clearSelection();
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.editorInput.focus();
      }
    
      handleDisplayMouseDown(e) {
        this.mouseDownOffset = this.getOffsetFromClick(e);
        this.selectionStart = this.mouseDownOffset;
        this.selectionEnd = this.mouseDownOffset;
        this.caretOffset = this.mouseDownOffset;
      }
    
      handleDisplayMouseMove(e) {
        if (typeof this.mouseDownOffset !== 'number') return;
        const offset = this.getOffsetFromClick(e);
        this.selectionEnd = offset;
        if (this.selectionStart > this.selectionEnd) {
          [this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd] = [this.selectionEnd, 
this.selectionStart];
        }
        this.caretOffset = offset;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      handleDisplayMouseUp(e) {
        delete this.mouseDownOffset;
      }
    
      getOffsetFromClick(e) {
        const rect = this.editorDisplay.getBoundingClientRect();
        const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
        const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
    
        const lines = this.text.split('\n');
        let offset = 0;
        const lineHeight = 
parseFloat(getComputedStyle(this.editorDisplay).lineHeight);
        const charWidth = 8; // Approximate, monospace font
    
        let lineNum = Math.floor((y + this.editorContent.scrollTop) / 
lineHeight);
        lineNum = Math.max(0, Math.min(lineNum, lines.length - 1));
    
        // Add all previous lines
        for (let i = 0; i < lineNum; i++) {
          offset += lines[i].length + 1; // +1 for newline
        }
    
        // Add position in current line
        const charPos = Math.round((x + this.editorContent.scrollLeft) / 
charWidth);
        const lineLength = lines[lineNum].length;
        offset += Math.max(0, Math.min(charPos, lineLength));
    
        return Math.min(offset, this.text.length);
      }
    
      insertChar(char) {
        if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {
          // Replace selection
          const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          const end = Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          this.text = this.text.slice(0, start) + char + this.text.slice(end);
          this.caretOffset = start + char.length;
          this.clearSelection();
        } else {
          // Insert at caret
          this.text = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset) + char + 
this.text.slice(this.caretOffset);
          this.caretOffset += char.length;
        }
    
        this.recordEdit();
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.updateLineNumbers();
        this.markModified();
      }
    
      backspace() {
        if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {
          // Delete selection
          const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          this.text = this.text.slice(0, start) + 
this.text.slice(Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd));
          this.caretOffset = start;
          this.clearSelection();
        } else if (this.caretOffset > 0) {
          this.text = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset - 1) + 
this.text.slice(this.caretOffset);
          this.caretOffset--;
        }
    
        this.recordEdit();
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.updateLineNumbers();
        this.markModified();
      }
    
      delete() {
        if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {
          const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          this.text = this.text.slice(0, start) + 
this.text.slice(Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd));
          this.caretOffset = start;
          this.clearSelection();
        } else if (this.caretOffset < this.text.length) {
          this.text = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset) + 
this.text.slice(this.caretOffset + 1);
        }
    
        this.recordEdit();
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.updateLineNumbers();
        this.markModified();
      }
    
      moveRight() {
        this.caretOffset = Math.min(this.caretOffset + 1, this.text.length);
        this.clearSelection();
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      moveLeft() {
        this.caretOffset = Math.max(this.caretOffset - 1, 0);
        this.clearSelection();
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      moveUp() {
        const lineStart = this.text.lastIndexOf('\n', this.caretOffset - 1) + 1;
        if (lineStart === 0) return; // Already on first line
    
        const colInLine = this.caretOffset - lineStart;
        const prevLineStart = this.text.lastIndexOf('\n', lineStart - 2) + 1;
        const prevLineEnd = lineStart - 1;
        const prevLineLength = prevLineEnd - prevLineStart;
    
        this.caretOffset = prevLineStart + Math.min(colInLine, prevLineLength);
        this.clearSelection();
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      moveDown() {
        const lineStart = this.text.lastIndexOf('\n', this.caretOffset - 1) + 1;
        const lineEnd = this.text.indexOf('\n', this.caretOffset);
        const actualLineEnd = lineEnd === -1 ? this.text.length : lineEnd;
    
        if (actualLineEnd === this.text.length) return; // Already on last line
    
        const colInLine = this.caretOffset - lineStart;
        const nextLineStart = actualLineEnd + 1;
        const nextLineEnd = this.text.indexOf('\n', nextLineStart);
        const actualNextLineEnd = nextLineEnd === -1 ? this.text.length : 
nextLineEnd;
        const nextLineLength = actualNextLineEnd - nextLineStart;
    
        this.caretOffset = nextLineStart + Math.min(colInLine, nextLineLength);
        this.clearSelection();
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      moveToLineStart(select) {
        const lineStart = this.text.lastIndexOf('\n', this.caretOffset - 1) + 1;
        if (select) {
          if (this.selectionStart === -1) {
            this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;
          }
          this.selectionEnd = lineStart;
        } else {
          this.clearSelection();
        }
        this.caretOffset = lineStart;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      moveToLineEnd(select) {
        const lineEnd = this.text.indexOf('\n', this.caretOffset);
        const actualLineEnd = lineEnd === -1 ? this.text.length : lineEnd;
        if (select) {
          if (this.selectionStart === -1) {
            this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;
          }
          this.selectionEnd = actualLineEnd;
        } else {
          this.clearSelection();
        }
        this.caretOffset = actualLineEnd;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      moveToNextWord(select) {
        let offset = this.caretOffset;
        // Skip current word
        while (offset < this.text.length && /\w/.test(this.text[offset])) 
offset++;
        // Skip whitespace
        while (offset < this.text.length && /\s/.test(this.text[offset])) 
offset++;
    
        if (select) {
          if (this.selectionStart === -1) {
            this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;
          }
          this.selectionEnd = offset;
        } else {
          this.clearSelection();
        }
        this.caretOffset = offset;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      moveToPrevWord(select) {
        let offset = this.caretOffset - 1;
        if (offset < 0) return;
    
        // Skip whitespace backward
        while (offset >= 0 && /\s/.test(this.text[offset])) offset--;
        // Skip word backward
        while (offset >= 0 && /\w/.test(this.text[offset])) offset--;
        offset++; // Back to start of word
    
        if (select) {
          if (this.selectionStart === -1) {
            this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;
          }
          this.selectionEnd = offset;
        } else {
          this.clearSelection();
        }
        this.caretOffset = offset;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      extendSelectionRight() {
        if (this.selectionStart === -1) {
          this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;
          this.selectionEnd = this.caretOffset + 1;
        } else {
          this.selectionEnd++;
        }
        this.caretOffset = this.selectionEnd;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      extendSelectionLeft() {
        if (this.selectionStart === -1) {
          this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;
          this.selectionEnd = this.caretOffset - 1;
        } else {
          this.selectionEnd--;
        }
        this.caretOffset = this.selectionEnd;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      extendSelectionUp() {
        // Complex - need to track column position
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      extendSelectionDown() {
        // Complex - need to track column position
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      selectAll() {
        this.selectionStart = 0;
        this.selectionEnd = this.text.length;
        this.caretOffset = this.text.length;
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      clearSelection() {
        this.selectionStart = -1;
        this.selectionEnd = -1;
      }
    
      copy() {
        if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {
          const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          const end = Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          const selectedText = this.text.slice(start, end);
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedText).catch(err => 
console.error('Copy failed:', err));
        }
      }
    
      cut() {
        if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {
          this.copy();
          const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          const end = Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
          this.text = this.text.slice(0, start) + this.text.slice(end);
          this.caretOffset = start;
          this.clearSelection();
          this.recordEdit();
          this.updateDisplay();
          this.updateLineNumbers();
          this.markModified();
        }
      }
    
      paste() {
        navigator.clipboard.readText()
          .then(text => this.insertChar(text))
          .catch(err => console.error('Paste failed:', err));
      }
    
      recordEdit() {
        if (this.isUndoRedo) {
          this.isUndoRedo = false;
          return;
        }
    
        const now = Date.now();
        const canGroup = this.lastAction === 'typing' && (now - 
this.lastActionTime) < 500;
    
        if (canGroup && this.historyIndex < this.history.length - 1) {
          // Replace the last history entry
          this.history[this.historyIndex] = this.text;
        } else {
          // Create new history entry
          this.history = this.history.slice(0, this.historyIndex + 1);
          this.history.push(this.text);
          this.historyIndex++;
        }
    
        this.lastAction = 'typing';
        this.lastActionTime = now;
    
        document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex <= 0;
        document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;
      }
    
      undo() {
        if (this.historyIndex > 0) {
          this.historyIndex--;
          this.isUndoRedo = true;
          this.text = this.history[this.historyIndex];
          this.clearSelection();
          this.updateDisplay();
          this.updateLineNumbers();
          this.markModified();
    
          document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex <= 0;
          document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = false;
        }
      }
    
      redo() {
        if (this.historyIndex < this.history.length - 1) {
          this.historyIndex++;
          this.isUndoRedo = true;
          this.text = this.history[this.historyIndex];
          this.clearSelection();
          this.updateDisplay();
          this.updateLineNumbers();
          this.markModified();
    
          document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = false;
          document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;
        }
      }
    
      updateDisplay() {
        const html = this.renderContent();
        this.editorDisplay.innerHTML = html;
      }
    
      renderContent() {
        let html = '';
        let offset = 0;
    
        for (let i = 0; i < this.text.length; i++) {
          const char = this.text[i];
          let charHtml = this.escapeHtml(char);
    
          // Check if this position is in selection
          if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) {
            const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
            const end = Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);
            if (i >= start && i < end) {
              charHtml = `<span class="selection">${charHtml}</span>`;
            }
          }
    
          // Check if this is a find match
          for (let m = 0; m < this.findMatches.length; m++) {
            const match = this.findMatches[m];
            if (i >= match.index && i < match.index + match.length) {
              const isCurrent = m === this.currentMatchIndex;
              charHtml = `<span class="find-match${isCurrent ? ' current' : 
''}">${charHtml}</span>`;
              break;
            }
          }
    
          // Add caret at this position
          if (i === this.caretOffset) {
            charHtml = `<span class="caret${this.caretVisible ? '' : ' 
hidden'}">${charHtml || '|'}</span>${charHtml ? '' : ''}`;
          }
    
          if (char === '\n') {
            html += '\n';
          } else if (char === '\t') {
            html += '  '; // Tab as spaces for display
          } else {
            html += charHtml;
          }
    
          offset++;
        }
    
        // Caret at end of text
        if (this.caretOffset === this.text.length) {
          html += `<span class="caret${this.caretVisible ? '' : ' 
hidden'}">|</span>`;
        }
    
        return html;
      }
    
      escapeHtml(text) {
        const map = {
          '&': '&amp;',
          '<': '&lt;',
          '>': '&gt;',
          '"': '&quot;',
          "'": '&#039;'
        };
        return text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => map[m]);
      }
    
      updateLineNumbers() {
        const lines = this.text.split('\n');
        let html = '';
        for (let i = 1; i <= lines.length; i++) {
          html += i + '\n';
        }
        this.lineNumbersEl.textContent = html;
      }
    
      updateCaretInfo() {
        const beforeCaret = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset);
        const lines = beforeCaret.split('\n');
        const lineNum = lines.length;
        const col = lines[lines.length - 1].length + 1;
    
        this.cursorInfoEl.textContent = `Line ${lineNum}, Column ${col}`;
      }
    
      updateFind() {
        const query = document.getElementById('findInput').value;
        if (!query) {
          this.findMatches = [];
          this.currentMatchIndex = -1;
          document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = '';
          this.updateDisplay();
          return;
        }
    
        const regex = new RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g');
        this.findMatches = [];
    
        let match;
        while ((match = regex.exec(this.text)) !== null) {
          this.findMatches.push({ index: match.index, length: match[0].length 
});
        }
    
        document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = 
          `${this.findMatches.length} match${this.findMatches.length !== 1 ? 
'es' : ''}`;
    
        this.updateDisplay();
      }
    
      findNext() {
        if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;
    
        this.currentMatchIndex = (this.currentMatchIndex + 1) % 
this.findMatches.length;
        const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];
        this.selectionStart = match.index;
        this.selectionEnd = match.index + match.length;
        this.caretOffset = match.index + match.length;
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.editorInput.focus();
      }
    
      findPrev() {
        if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;
    
        this.currentMatchIndex = (this.currentMatchIndex - 1 + 
this.findMatches.length) % this.findMatches.length;
        const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];
        this.selectionStart = match.index;
        this.selectionEnd = match.index + match.length;
        this.caretOffset = match.index + match.length;
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.editorInput.focus();
      }
    
      replaceCurrent() {
        if (this.currentMatchIndex === -1 || this.findMatches.length === 0) 
return;
    
        const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];
        const replaceText = document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;
    
        this.text = this.text.slice(0, match.index) + replaceText + 
this.text.slice(match.index + match.length);
        this.caretOffset = match.index + replaceText.length;
        this.clearSelection();
        this.recordEdit();
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.updateLineNumbers();
        this.markModified();
    
        this.updateFind();
        if (this.findMatches.length > 0) {
          this.findNext();
        }
      }
    
      replaceAll() {
        if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;
    
        const query = document.getElementById('findInput').value;
        const replaceText = document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;
    
        this.text = this.text.replace(new RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g'), 
replaceText);
        this.clearSelection();
        this.recordEdit();
        this.updateDisplay();
        this.updateLineNumbers();
        this.markModified();
    
        this.updateFind();
      }
    
      escapeRegex(string) {
        return string.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
      }
    
      toggleHistory() {
        const panel = document.getElementById('historyPanel');
        if (panel.style.display === 'none') {
          panel.style.display = 'flex';
          this.displayHistory();
        } else {
          panel.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }
    
      displayHistory() {
        const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
        historyList.innerHTML = '';
    
        const revisions = this.currentDocument.revisions || [];
        revisions.forEach(rev => {
          const item = document.createElement('div');
          item.className = 'history-item';
    
          const revNum = document.createElement('div');
          revNum.className = 'history-item-revision';
          revNum.textContent = `Revision ${rev.revision}`;
    
          const timestamp = document.createElement('div');
          timestamp.className = 'history-item-timestamp';
          const date = new Date(rev.timestamp);
          timestamp.textContent = date.toLocaleString();
    
          const buttons = document.createElement('div');
          buttons.className = 'history-item-buttons';
    
          const previewBtn = document.createElement('button');
          previewBtn.className = 'btn btn-sm';
          previewBtn.textContent = 'Preview';
          previewBtn.addEventListener('click', () => 
this.previewRevision(rev.revision));
    
          const restoreBtn = document.createElement('button');
          restoreBtn.className = 'btn btn-sm';
          restoreBtn.textContent = 'Restore';
          restoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => 
this.restoreRevision(rev.revision));
    
          buttons.appendChild(previewBtn);
          buttons.appendChild(restoreBtn);
    
          item.appendChild(revNum);
          item.appendChild(timestamp);
          item.appendChild(buttons);
    
          historyList.appendChild(item);
        });
      }
    
      async previewRevision(revision) {
        try {
          const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/revisions/${revision}`);
          const data = await response.json();
    
          const modal = document.getElementById('previewModal');
          const content = document.getElementById('previewContent');
          content.textContent = data.content;
          modal.style.display = 'flex';
        } catch (err) {
          this.showError('Failed to load revision');
        }
      }
    
      restoreRevision(revision) {
        const closePreview = () => {
          document.getElementById('previewModal').style.display = 'none';
        };
    
        fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/revisions/${revision}`)
          .then(r => r.json())
          .then(data => {
            closePreview();
            this.text = data.content;
            this.recordEdit();
            this.caretOffset = 0;
            this.clearSelection();
            this.updateDisplay();
            this.updateLineNumbers();
            this.markModified();
            this.showMessage(`Restored revision ${revision} as unsaved 
changes`);
          })
          .catch(err => this.showError('Failed to restore revision'));
      }
    
      async save() {
        if (this.syncInProgress) return;
    
        const content = this.text;
        const currentDoc = this.currentDocument.content;
    
        if (content === currentDoc) {
          this.showMessage('No changes to save');
          return;
        }
    
        this.syncInProgress = true;
        document.getElementById('saveBtn').disabled = true;
    
        try {
          const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}/save`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              content,
              baseRevision: this.baseRevision,
              documentId: this.currentDocument.id
            })
          });
    
          const data = await response.json();
    
          if (response.status === 409) {
            this.showConflict(data.currentRevision);
            return;
          }
    
          if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed');
          }
    
          this.currentDocument.currentRevision = data.revision;
          this.baseRevision = data.revision;
          this.currentDocument.content = content;
          this.revisionEl.textContent = `Revision ${data.revision}`;
          this.markSaved();
          this.showMessage('Saved successfully');
    
          this.loadDocument();
        } catch (err) {
          this.showError(err.message || 'Save failed');
        } finally {
          this.syncInProgress = false;
          document.getElementById('saveBtn').disabled = false;
        }
      }
    
      async reloadDocument() {
        try {
          const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${this.currentDocument.id}`);
          const doc = await response.json();
    
          this.currentDocument = doc;
          this.baseRevision = doc.currentRevision;
          this.text = doc.content;
          this.caretOffset = 0;
          this.clearSelection();
          this.updateDisplay();
          this.updateLineNumbers();
          this.markSaved();
    
          document.getElementById('conflictModal').style.display = 'none';
          this.showMessage('Document reloaded from server');
        } catch (err) {
          this.showError('Failed to reload document');
        }
      }
    
      showConflict(serverRevision) {
        this.saveStatusEl.classList.add('error');
        this.statusEl.textContent = 'Conflict: Save rejected';
        document.getElementById('conflictModal').style.display = 'flex';
      }
    
      markModified() {
        this.isModified = true;
        this.saveStatusEl.classList.add('unsaved');
        this.statusEl.textContent = 'Unsaved changes';
      }
    
      markSaved() {
        this.isModified = false;
        this.saveStatusEl.classList.remove('unsaved', 'error');
        this.statusEl.textContent = 'Saved';
      }
    
      startCaretBlink() {
        if (this.caretBlinkInterval) return;
    
        this.caretBlinkInterval = setInterval(() => {
          this.caretVisible = !this.caretVisible;
          this.updateDisplay();
        }, 500);
      }
    
      showMessage(message) {
        const statusBar = document.getElementById('statusBar');
        statusBar.textContent = message;
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (statusBar.textContent === message) {
            statusBar.textContent = '';
          }
        }, 3000);
      }
    
      showError(message) {
        const statusBar = document.getElementById('statusBar');
        statusBar.textContent = '✗ ' + message;
        statusBar.style.color = 'var(--color-error)';
        setTimeout(() => {
          statusBar.textContent = '';
          statusBar.style.color = '';
        }, 5000);
      }
    }
    
    // Initialize editor
    let editor;
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      editor = new TextEditor();
    });
    
    function closePreview() {
      document.getElementById('previewModal').style.display = 'none';
    }

this.selectionEnd++;\n    }\n    this.caretOffset = this.selectionEnd;\n

(this.selectionStart === -1) {\n      this.selectionStart = this.caretOffset;\n 
this.selectionEnd = this.caretOffset - 1;\n    } else {\n      
this.selectionEnd--;\n    }\n    this.caretOffset = this.selectionEnd;\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  extendSelectionUp() {\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  extendSelectionDown() {\n    
this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  selectAll() {\n    this.selectionStart = 0;\n   
this.selectionEnd = this.text.length;\n    this.caretOffset = 
this.text.length;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  clearSelection() {\n    
this.selectionStart = -1;\n    this.selectionEnd = -1;\n  }\n\n  copy() {\n

= Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      const end = 
Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      const selectedText = 
this.text.slice(start, end);\n      
navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedText).catch(err => console.error('Copy 
failed:', err));\n    }\n  }\n\n  cut() {\n    if (this.selectionStart !== -1 &&
this.selectionEnd !== -1) {\n      this.copy();\n      const start = 
Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      const end = 
Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n      this.text = 
this.text.slice(0, start) + this.text.slice(end);\n      this.caretOffset = 
start;\n      this.clearSelection();\n      this.recordEdit();\n      
this.updateDisplay();\n      this.updateLineNumbers();\n      
this.markModified();\n    }\n  }\n\n  paste() {\n    
navigator.clipboard.readText()\n      .then(text => this.insertChar(text))\n    
.catch(err => console.error('Paste failed:', err));\n  }\n\n  recordEdit() {\n  
if (this.isUndoRedo) {\n      this.isUndoRedo = false;\n      return;\n    }\n\n
const now = Date.now();\n    const canGroup = this.lastAction === 'typing' && 
(now - this.lastActionTime) < 500;\n\n    if (canGroup && this.historyIndex < 
this.history.length - 1) {\n      this.history[this.historyIndex] = this.text;\n
} else {\n      this.history = this.history.slice(0, this.historyIndex + 1);\n  
this.history.push(this.text);\n      this.historyIndex++;\n    }\n\n    
this.lastAction = 'typing';\n    this.lastActionTime = now;\n\n    
document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex <= 0;\n    
document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;\n  }\n\n  undo() {\n    if (this.historyIndex > 0) {\n 
this.historyIndex--;\n      this.isUndoRedo = true;\n      this.text = 
this.history[this.historyIndex];\n      this.clearSelection();\n      
this.updateDisplay();\n      this.updateLineNumbers();\n      
this.markModified();\n\n      document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = 
this.historyIndex <= 0;\n      document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = 
false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  redo() {\n    if (this.historyIndex < 
this.history.length - 1) {\n      this.historyIndex++;\n      this.isUndoRedo = 
true;\n      this.text = this.history[this.historyIndex];\n      
this.clearSelection();\n      this.updateDisplay();\n      
this.updateLineNumbers();\n      this.markModified();\n\n      
document.getElementById('undoBtn').disabled = false;\n      
document.getElementById('redoBtn').disabled = this.historyIndex >= 
this.history.length - 1;\n    }\n  }\n\n  updateDisplay() {\n    const lines = 
this.text.split('\\n');\n    let html = '';\n\n    for (let lineIdx = 0; lineIdx
< lines.length; lineIdx++) {\n      const line = lines[lineIdx];\n      const 
lineStartOffset = lines.slice(0, lineIdx).reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.length + 1,
0);\n\n      for (let charIdx = 0; charIdx < line.length; charIdx++) {\n        
const offset = lineStartOffset + charIdx;\n        const char = line[charIdx];\n
let charHtml = this.escapeHtml(char);\n\n        if (char === '\\t') {\n        
charHtml = '<span class=\"tab\">  </span>';\n        }\n\n        // Check if in
selection\n        if (this.selectionStart !== -1 && this.selectionEnd !== -1) 
{\n          const start = Math.min(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n  
const end = Math.max(this.selectionStart, this.selectionEnd);\n          if 
(offset >= start && offset < end) {\n            charHtml = `<span 
class=\"selection\">${charHtml}</span>`;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // 
Check if find match\n        for (let m = 0; m < this.findMatches.length; m++) 
{\n          const match = this.findMatches[m];\n          if (offset >= 
match.index && offset < match.index + match.length) {\n            const 
isCurrent = m === this.currentMatchIndex;\n            charHtml = `<span 
class=\"find-match${isCurrent ? ' current' : ''}\">${charHtml}</span>`;\n       
break;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // Add caret\n        if (offset === 
this.caretOffset && this.caretVisible) {\n          charHtml = `<span 
class=\"caret\">${charHtml}</span>`;\n        }\n\n        html += charHtml;\n  
}\n\n      // Caret at end of line\n      if (this.caretOffset === 
lineStartOffset + line.length && this.caretVisible) {\n        html += '<span 
class=\"caret\">|</span>';\n      }\n\n      if (lineIdx < lines.length - 1) {\n
html += '\\n';\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Caret at very end\n    if 
(this.text.length === 0 && this.caretOffset === 0 && this.caretVisible) {\n     
html = '<span class=\"caret\">|</span>';\n    }\n\n    
this.editorDisplay.innerHTML = html;\n  }\n\n  escapeHtml(text) {\n    const map
= { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '\"': '&quot;', \"'\": '&#039;' };\n
return text.replace(/[&<>\"']/g, m => map[m]);\n  }\n\n  updateLineNumbers() {\n
const lines = this.text.split('\\n');\n    let html = '';\n    for (let i = 1; i
<= lines.length; i++) {\n      html += i + '\\n';\n    }\n    
this.lineNumbersEl.textContent = html;\n  }\n\n  updateCaretInfo() {\n    const 
beforeCaret = this.text.slice(0, this.caretOffset);\n    const lines = 
beforeCaret.split('\\n');\n    const lineNum = lines.length;\n    const col = 
lines[lines.length - 1].length + 1;\n\n    this.cursorInfoEl.textContent = `Line
${lineNum}, Column ${col}`;\n  }\n\n  updateFind() {\n    const query = 
document.getElementById('findInput').value;\n    if (!query) {\n      
this.findMatches = [];\n      this.currentMatchIndex = -1;\n      
document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = '';\n      
this.updateDisplay();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const regex = new 
RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g');\n    this.findMatches = [];\n\n    let 
match;\n    while ((match = regex.exec(this.text)) !== null) {\n      
this.findMatches.push({ index: match.index, length: match[0].length });\n    
}\n\n    document.getElementById('matchCount').textContent = \n      
`${this.findMatches.length} match${this.findMatches.length !== 1 ? 'es' : 
''}`;\n\n    this.updateDisplay();\n  }\n\n  findNext() {\n    if 
(this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    this.currentMatchIndex = 
(this.currentMatchIndex + 1) % this.findMatches.length;\n    const match = 
this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];\n    this.selectionStart = 
match.index;\n    this.selectionEnd = match.index + match.length;\n    
this.caretOffset = match.index + match.length;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.editorInput.focus();\n  }\n\n  findPrev() {\n    if 
(this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    this.currentMatchIndex = 
(this.currentMatchIndex - 1 + this.findMatches.length) % 
this.findMatches.length;\n    const match = 
this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];\n    this.selectionStart = 
match.index;\n    this.selectionEnd = match.index + match.length;\n    
this.caretOffset = match.index + match.length;\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.editorInput.focus();\n  }\n\n  replaceCurrent() {\n    if 
(this.currentMatchIndex === -1 || this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    
const match = this.findMatches[this.currentMatchIndex];\n    const replaceText =
document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;\n\n    this.text = 
this.text.slice(0, match.index) + replaceText + this.text.slice(match.index + 
match.length);\n    this.caretOffset = match.index + replaceText.length;\n    
this.clearSelection();\n    this.recordEdit();\n    this.updateDisplay();\n    
this.updateLineNumbers();\n    this.markModified();\n\n    this.updateFind();\n 
if (this.findMatches.length > 0) {\n      this.findNext();\n    }\n  }\n\n  
replaceAll() {\n    if (this.findMatches.length === 0) return;\n\n    const 
query = document.getElementById('findInput').value;\n    const replaceText = 
document.getElementById('replaceInput').value;\n\n    this.text = 
this.text.replace(new RegExp(this.escapeRegex(query), 'g'), replaceText);\n

}\n\n  escapeRegex(string) {\n    return 
string.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, '\\\\$&');\n  }\n\n  toggleHistory()

this.text;\n    const currentDoc = this.currentDocument.content;\n\n    if
